PCW — Pay-per-view

Decimation 2002, 22 December 2002

22 December 2002
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Decimation 2002 22 Dec 2002 The Toronto Skydome Opening video package: Six title matches and revenge at Decimation The PCW and RUSH logos flash up onto the screen before ramming together, sending sparks flying and “Obscure Thing” by Ash starts, up, swirling guitars leading into the thumping body of the song as PCW and RUSH stars in action fade in then out from the sparks. MANY REASONS TO FIGHT.... The ending of Alliances is shown as Xavier Kannon holds his arms aloft in the snow. FOR KING AND COUNTRY.... Mark Amulet and Mark Michigan are shown staring at one another, the King’s robe between them. FOR GOLD AND GLORY..... Matt Hunt, Gideon Poe, Jon Kano and Teresa Tomas are shown along with the PCW TV and RUSH Network Titles. FOR REVENGE..... The smirking face of Gaz Green looks at the snarling Nerezza. FOR SOVEREIGNTY AND HONOUR..... The 4 faces of Marvin Harris, El Spiffy!, Stimpo and Ebola III are shown around the RUSH Syndicate Title. FOR A PRIZE AT THE END OF A WAR.... Snow, Rush, Chet King, TTT, The End and Dante Jackson are shown battling each other around the Continental Title. FOR BRAGGING RIGHTS.... Xavier Kannon and Duke Williams are seen staring across a desk at one another. TO BE THE KING OF THE MOUNTAIN.... We are shown both Tyler Valentine and McGlocklan holding the RUSH Title they’ll fight for. AND FOR THE RESPECT OF ALL..... El Diablo is seen staring longingly at the PCW World Title under the gaze of Clyde as we zoom in on the Title’s crest and the screen is drenched in bright lights where we cut to the interior of the Toronto Skydome, pyro exploding all around the building. Welcome to Decimation from the Toronto Skydome Dave: HELLO AND WELCOME TO DECIMATION, THE FINAL PAY PER VIEW OF WHAT HAS BEEN A HUGE YEAR FOR PCW!!! The camera circles high around the Dome, showing the ring exploding with pyro and 2 announce desks set up at ringside where we zoom in on one to show Dave Kern, Jeff Marx and PCW’s Commissioner Ellie~! Dave: We are live from the Toronto Skydome, with a MASSIVE PCW and RUSH card for you all, with Titles on the line and rivalries to be settled. Jeff: And you’re forgetting the smiting of Duke and all his Redneck morons! Eleanor: Like... SO yeah! Dave: We’re here to call the PCW action, and beside us the RUSH announcers are all raring to bring their action to you all! The camera switches to the adjacent desk where Argyle, Kris Raves and Vanilla Skillz are seated, ready for the 1st match. Raves: I wanna be at there table! Argyle: Stop it now! Skillz: Yo sho be RUSH an’ proud, homeslice! Argyle: As you’ve been told, we have a stacked card of matches, but it all has to start somewhere and that’s NOW!!! Raves: The crappy opener slot RUSH deserves! Matt Elia vs Mr. Uk Argyle: This is going to be a LONG night... Anyway, ladies and gents, to our FIRST match-up of the evening, pitting the BOOBIE BOY~! of the RUSH Resistance against the infamous CRUMPET~! King of the United Kingdom! That’s right, Matt Elia versus Mr. Uk! Kris Raves: “Oh toodle my fucking noodle, I have to suffer through this already?” Vanilla Skillz: “Yo dawg, I finished watching ‘Menace Too Society’ and I’m just waiting for Raves’s crack head ass to offer me some cheeseburgers, man.” From out of nowhere, “God Save the Queen” by Godsmack cues up over the P.A. system to an uproarious chorus of boos from the audience. They know all too well about the man who then emerges from the backstage curtains, Mr. Uk. With a smug look painted across his face, he struts down the ramp toward ringside. Clay: “The following match is scheduled for one fall with an undisclosed time limit. Introducing first, from London, England and weighing in at 237 lbs., he is RUSH’s undisputed KING of CRUMPETS~! He is……MR. UKKKKKKKKKKKKKK!!!” The crowd’s negative reaction only intensifies as Uk slides in the ring and poses on the turnbuckles. He soaks it all up, loving every ounce of their hate. Kris Raves: “Now there’s a man who knows how to take things in stride.” Argyle: “Mr. Uk is a sycophant and a psychopath. I’m hardly surprised you admire him.” Vanilla Skillz: “I have no idea what Argie just said, but it sounded like a dis on Raves so ‘Oooooo!’ You gonna let him down you like that, dawg?” Meanwhile, Uk’s theme fades out into oblivion and after a brief interlude of silence is replaced by “Banned From the Back Porch” by Saves the Day along with a deafening “BOOBIE BOY~!” chant from a positively ecstatic crowd. As if he’d been drawn out only by their chants, none other than Matt Elia emerges from backstage and with a big smile on his face runs down the ramp way slapping the outstretched hands of numerous fans along the way. Clay: “And his opponent, from Boston, Massachusetts and weighing in at a trim 205 lbs., you know him and love him as the BOOBIE BOY~!, here is...MATTTT ELIA!” Argyle & Skillz: “BOOOOOOOOOOBBBBBIIIIEEEEE BOY~!” Kris Raves: “You two realize you’re complete morons, yes?” Elia slides into the ring and goes up top to play to the fans some more, but is suddenly cut off as Uk grabs his trunks from behind and sends him tumbling onto the canvas. The fans ‘boo’ out their disapproval as Referee Bob Charlie severely chastises Mr. Uk verbally for the pre-match assault. Kris Raves: “HA! Now that’s what I like to see, a good ole fashion sneak attack.” Argyle: “No doubt a sentiment that Mr. Uk shares with you, Kris. However, I doubt he’ll catch Elia twice with the same trap.” Vanilla Skillz: “Yeah, dawg, Matt Elia’s got almost as much skillz as me, almost. Shame he’s a white boy or else he’d a part of my crew!” Kris Raves: “Don’t you get it yet? YOU’RE WHITE TOO!” Vanilla Skillz: “Yo, Kris, why you trippin’?” The bell rings and the match officially starts, Uk and Elia sizing each other up in the ring center before locking up and wrestling around until Uk gains the advantage and manages to grab Elia in a reverse headlock and drop him down for a quick DDT. Elia rolls around on the canvas holding his head as Uk stands over him. Argyle: “Hard hitting move by Uk there, he’s obviously trying to wound Elia as badly as he can early on in the match so he can pick up the easy win.” Kris Raves: “He ‘picked up’ the easy win when he stepped into the ring with that idiot Elia. Anyone beaten up by a girl doesn’t deserve to set foot in MY wrestling ring.” Vanilla Skillz: “That’s a shame, dawg. How you gonna be stepping in your own ring when yo momma beat yo ass on a regular basis?” As Argyle and Skillz laugh at the seemingly enraged Raves, Uk sets Elia up in a standing head scissors and then lifts him up high, only to slam him back down again with a picture perfect Powerbomb. Again, Elia rolls around in pain on the canvas until Uk jumps down and hooks the leg. 1… 2… KICKOUT! Elia wisely turns his entire body on its side and ends the pin attempt. A very annoyed Mr. Uk then begins pulling Elia up by the hair back onto his feet. Argyle: “Elia is taking a beating here, I just hope he’s conserving his energy to catch Uk when he wears himself out.” Kris Raves: “That is possibly the weakest defense for a man getting his ass kicked that I’ve ever heard.” Vanilla Skillz: “Come on BOOBIE BOY~! Break a crumpet off in that foo’s ass, dawg!” Now back on his feet, Elia and Uk begin trading hard right hands to the face. This goes on for a good moment or two before Uk catches Elia’s hand and turns it around into a British Armbar. Matt Elia winces hard as Uk wrenches on the arm, but suddenly rolls out of it and catches a surprised Uk with a Short arm Clothesline. Argyle: “Beautiful reversal by Elia there! I’m sure that took Mr. Uk by surprise.” Kris Raves: “Tis but a minor hiccup in the inevitable jobbing of Matt Elia, fear not.” Vanilla Skillz: “It goes one for the treble, two for the bass-..” Quickly recovering from the clothesline, Uk and Elia are brawling with one another again in no time at all. Finally, they manage to get into a lock-up and a faster Elia whips around to the side and attempts a Russian Leg Sweep, but the crafty Mr. Uk quickly reverses him by bending his knees and grabbing Elia in a side waist-lock which he turns to a Fallaway Slam, planting Elia back first onto the mat. Argyle: “Uk basically snubbing Elia by pulling off a reversal of his own, I see no love lost between these two here tonight.” Kris Raves: “Actually, Uk just wanted to show the ingrate kids how to actually pull one off, he’s just trying to help.” Vanilla Skillz: “-Kris got DES NUTZ all over his face!” With both men back on the canvas and Elia still dazed, Uk goes for another cover. Referee Charlie hastily slides down and begins the count. 1… 2… 3NO! At the last second Elia manages to jerk his shoulder up. An angered Mr. Uk leans up and begins actually throttling Matt Elia, trying to choke him out. Bob Charlie quickly tries to break the two apart. Argyle: “Bad form! Uk, like a child throwing a temper tantrum, resorts to choking when he doesn’t get his way.” Kris Raves: “Don’t stop him! If anything he’s doing us a favor! Come on; think about it, one less Neanderthal jackass taking up anymore of my precious air supply.” Vanilla Skillz: “-He goes; six, five, four, and three is up…Gimmie dat, gimmie dat, gimmie dat nu--“ Kris Raves: “-What the hell are you babbling about over there?” Vanilla Skillz: “Don’t hate, Kris, it’s my latest rap. I call it; ‘Kris Raves is a dumbass white boy who can’t get laid (Mad Skillz remix)’…Whatcha think, yo?” Argyle: “I think we should get back to the match.” Bob Charlie manages to get Uk off of Elia before he’s able to choke to unconsciousness, and then tries to give Uk a stern lecture which the CRUMPET~! King promptly ignores. In the mean time, Elia quickly climbs to his feet and then up the turnbuckles while Uk’s back is turned. The referee notices just in time to dodge out of the way as Elia leaps off and catches Uk behind the head with a Missile Dropkick that sends him tumbling out of the ring and onto the apron. Argyle: “I hate to cite something as ethereal as karma, but what else could have ordained Uk taking a shot like that right after trying to score a cheap win?” Kris Raves: “Blind, simple, doo-da, clueless LUCK! Nothing more.” Vanilla Skillz: “What’s up wit dat? I thought you liked a good sneak-attack, Raves?” An exhausted Elia lays face down on the canvas while a severely dazed Uk is likewise down on the outside. After a moment to survey the carnage, Referee Bob Charlie very slowly begins the double count. 1… 2… 3… 4… 5… Elia slowly forces himself to his feet. However, he doesn’t notice Uk until the last second as he climbs up onto the ring’s edge and suddenly springboards off to catch Elia from the back with a Springboard Dropkick, sending both men down like a sack of bricks. Argyle: “The way these men trade off moves nearly identical is proof of how much they really just hate each other, trying to show one another up as the better competitor.” Kris Raves: “I saw it more as Elia was botching and Uk was trying to teach the punk how to do the moves correctly, but fuck it I’ll leave that one to interpretation.” Vanilla Skillz: “Dope way to start the show though, dawg.” Both men fearing a cover quickly slump over onto the ropes, slowly pulling themselves to their feet before they realize the proximity of the other. Though tired, they engage in one last round of brawling punches to the cranium. Mr. Uk, being the quicker to regain his senses, suddenly drops down and nails Elia hard with the Low-Blow! The crowd goes positively ape shit with boos toward Uk as Elia slumps down in excruciating pain. Argyle: “I don’t know what he was trying to ‘teach’ Elia there, Raves, but it sure as hell wasn’t legal.” Kris Raves: “The best ways of learning life’s lessons often aren’t.” Vanilla Skillz: “That’s low, dawg, even for Uk. Some things just ain’t meant to be taking the hit, ya feel me?” Uk wants to go for a pin, but Bob Charlie has other ideas, getting in Uk’s face and telling him it’s his ‘last warning’ being one of them. Rolling his eyes at the threat, Uk nods in a less than heartfelt showing of capitulation and then goes for the cover, which Bob Charlie does go down to count. 1… 2… 3…NO! Out of the corner of his eye Bob Charlie notices Elia’s foot hooked around the bottom rope, and stops the count. Uk, his eyes wide with rage, quickly gets to his feet and begins calling Bob Charlie every curse in the book. Argyle: “Another near fall for Uk! Though really he shouldn’t be allowed even that after what he did to Elia.” Kris Raves: “Blah, blah, blah! A win’s a win.” Vanilla Skillz: “I think we need to get this crowd up in here to make some noise! Come on, say it wit me, BOOBIE BOY~!” As if waiting for such a catalyst the crowd goes into a near-unison chant of “BOOBIE BOY~!” that echoes throughout the ring. The only one seemingly not pleased is Mr. Uk who begins leaning off the ropes and screaming “SHUT UP!” back toward the crowd. This, of course, is the last distraction Elia needs as he climbs up to his feet, suddenly grabs Uk in a reverse DDT position, then spins him around in a fluid motion drops Uk and nails a knife-edge chop to the neck! Argyle & Skillz: “BOOBSHAKALAKA~!” Kris Raves: “Oh fuck me, NO!” Uk is completely taken for a loop as Elia climbs over top of him and makes the pin. 1… 2… 3!!! Clay: THE WINNER OF THIS MATCH....... MATT ELIA!!! Raves: Eugh, I have an awful taste in my mouth, please, let us have some PCW action... NOW! Jeff: Kris ROCKS~! Dave: Well, on the RUSH side of things, Elia has chalked up a win for the RUSH resistance, and now it’s PCW’s turn as we have the battle of the Marks... Amulet and Michigan. Jeff: The irony being, neither have Marks. Eleanor: ELLIE~! HAS MARKS.. YAY ME!!! Jeff: I mark for you.... And lust after... Dave: AHEM! This rivalry has been going for a while, but this time the stakes are higher as nothing less than the King’s robe is on the line. Jeff: Well, if you don’t have a Title you have to fight for something I guess. Dave: *Sigh* Over to Clay. Mark Amulet vs Mark Michigan Clay: THE FOLLOWING IS A LADDER MATCH FOR KING POLLE’S ROBE! Eleanor: Ellie wants a robe! INTRODUCING FIRST... WEIGHING IN AT 267 POUNDS, FROM ANN ARBOR, MICHIGAN, MARK MICHIGAN!! 'Baby's Got A Temper' by Prodigy begins to play as Michigan comes out from the back to pyros shooting off in his honor and his music blasting. He ignores the jeers of the crowd as he makes his way to the ring in a slow and steady manner. Jeff: Oh my god it's the battle of the Mark's once again! Didn't we get enough of this crap last PPV? Dave: Obviously not. Clay: HIS OPPONENT, WEIGHING IN AT 238 POUNDS, FROM WINDSOR ENGLAND, BEING ACCOMPANIED BY KING POLLE HIMSELF, MARK AMULET! "Kult" by Amulet blares on and the crowd gives a good pop. A bunch of red lights make a pathway to the ring in the red carpet type effect as Amulet and King Polle emerge from the back. They walk down the ramp soaking in the cheer as Michigan looks on in disdain. Jeff: Oh come on. They aren't that fucking special! Ellie~!: Like I never get the red carpet when I come out and I am way important. Dave: You just keep thinking that. Amulet rolls into the ring and Michigan instantly goes on the attack, kicking Amulet repeatedly keeping him to the mat. Michigan pulls Amulet up and hits him hard in the chest with a chop. He backs him into the ropes and sends him to the other side. As Amulet comes back, Michigan bends over slightly picking Amulet up onto his shoulders releasing him with a samoan drop. Dave: Michigan is owning this match from the get go. Jeff: That's because he has got brains. Ellie~!: My Xavi's got brains. Dave: Well isn't that nice. (sarcastic) Michigan picks Amulet up and King Polle starts pounding on the ring apron trying to gain support for Amulet. Michigan scoop slams Amulet to the mat arrogantly. He then makes a yawning gesture with his hand as he goes for the jumping elbow, but Amulet rolls out of the way! The crowd cheers as Amulet gets to his feet and sends a ferocious blend of left and right blows to the jaw of Michigan. Amulet grabs Michigan by the arm, tossing him into the ropes. As Michigan bounces back he is brought down by a drop toe hold perfectly executed by Amulet. Amulet then stands and drives an elbow right into the spine of Michigan who feels the pain instantly. Ellie~!: Like that has so got to hurt. Jeff: If you are a strong man like myself then that kind of thing doesn't even phase you. Dave: Jeff you cry over a needle. Ellie~!: A needle? Jeff: No I don't. Dave reaches into his pocket and pulls out a needle and pricks Jeff with it. Jeff screeches and looks down at his finger which has a small drop of blood causing him to faint. Dave: See what a wimp. Back in the ring Amulet has Michigan in the corner driving shoulders into his sternum. He then takes a few steps back as Michigan is just hanging there and hits him with a superkick to the head which causes Michigan to slump forward and hit the mat face first. Amulet turns around to see King Polle trying to roll in the ladder with little effect. Amulet walks over and helps him pull it the rest of the way in and drags it to the center of the ring. Amulet tries to set the ladder up, but from behind comes Michigan who hits him in the back of the head with an elbow. Michigan then grabs Amulet punching him a few more times, and takes him over to the turnbuckles where he sits him down. Michigan then walks over to the ladder and sets it vertical towards the turnbuckle. Jeff: Yes! It's time for some hardcore shit! Dave: This is what will make you or break you in PCW. Because PCW is all about blood and guts! Ellie~!: Like ewwzies to blood and guts. Besides my Xavi bear says it's all about the money. Jeff: Right he is. Michigan climbs to the top rope with Amulet and lifts him up. He then sets up for the suplex, but before he can pull it off, Amulet blocks it and tries to push Michigan off the top rope. Michigan falls back and as he does he grabs hold of Amulet and manages to monkey flip him from the top rope onto the ladder before hitting the mat! Jeff: Now that's what I call entertainment. Ellie~!: Like so not entertainment! Xavi in a match is entertaining. Xavi in bed is.... Dave: Please stop!! Ellie~!: Like stop what? Dave: Stop referring to your egotistical husband, and leave all personal time stories to yourself. Jeff: I personally want to hear about it. Dave: Only because you get off on other people's sex stories due to the fact that you lack a sex life. Michigan rolls the motionless body off of the ladder as he sets it up. He climbs onto the first rung and King Polle looks highly stressed as he is yelling his lungs off to get Amulet up. Michigan gets to the second rung and looks down and sees Amulet starting to stir, so he jumps down and pulls Amulet to his feet. Michigan Irish whips Amulet to the ropes, and brings him down with a sidewalk slam. Michigan, wanting to get this match over with, then leaps onto the ladder and skips several rungs trying to reach for the robe, which dangles over the ring. He is only a few feet away but his ascension is stopped by Amulet who comes up from behind pulling Michigan off by his right foot. Amulet whirls him around and back body drops him. He picks him up and puts his head between his legs ready for a powerbomb or other type of maneuver. Amulet lifts Michigan up but before he can bring him back down, Michigan manages to get his feet back on the ground, and reverses it. Instead of dropping Amulet he holds on to his legs and then pulls him forward driving him hard to the mat. Dave: This has been a straight on match for the most part, nothing real special about it. Ellie~!: Don't they need to like utilize the ladder more? Dave and Jeff look at Ellie~! who just broke out with a big word. Ellie~!: Like I have been watching the Learning Channel and stuff. As if overhearing this, Michigan folds the ladder back up and drops it on top of Amulet! Amulet lets out a loud moan as it drives into his arm which was protecting his stomach area. Michigan then aligns the ladder along Amulet's body and climbs to the top rope. Michigan jumps off and hits a frog splash. Dave: That move brought some pain to Michigan, but it seems effective against Amulet as well. Jessie: I am guessing….better yet I am hoping Amulet has a few cracked ribs. Michigan slowly starts to move and pulls the ladder off of Amulet once again. Michigan tries to pull it up once but drops it, the second he try he manages to pull it up and ultimately set it up. King Polle is over by Amulet desperately trying to get him moving as Michigan starts his way up the ladder. The higher Michigan gets, the louder the crowd boos. He steps up one more rung and manages to get his fingertips on the rope! Dave: This could be it. Jeff: Get it Michigan! Ellie~!: Like is this exciting or what? Michigan goes to climb up the next step, and as he does the boos turn into cheers. Michigan turns his head and sees Amulet on the top rope. Amulet jumps off and dropkicks Michigan off of the ladder causing him to land neck first onto the ropes which has a whiplash effect tossing Michigan back into the ladder and knocking it onto the ropes as he hits the mat. King Polle is jumping up and down as Amulet pulls himself up with the aid of the ropes. Amulet slowly walks over to the ladder, and pulls it back into position with Michigan directly under it. Amulet starts to slowly advance up the ladder as the crowd, as well as King Polle, cheer him on. Amulet is inches away from the robe. Michigan starts to come to however and he looks up and sees that his defeat is almost near. With all his might, Michigan pushes on to ladder. Dave: If Michigan pushes that ladder he is going to send Amulet flying into the stands! That could just about kill him! Jeff: We don't need anymore lawsuits. Ellie~!: Like this is the climax right here!! I am so scared. Jeff: I'll protect you. Ellie~!: Like ew! Michigan pushes again and the ladder falls to the side. Amulet sensed that coming, so before it falls over he leaps up and grabs hold of the robe!! As he is hanging their clutching on the robe for dear life, it breaks off of the cable holding it, and Amulet falls from several feet up onto Michigan!! Crowd: Holy Shit Eh!! Holy Shit Eh!! Dave: Amulet has just won the match!! Amulet has won, but both men look seriously injured. Clay: THE WINNER OF THIS MATCH..... MARK AMULET! Eleanor: Now they should, like, have to give me the robe and stuff.... Because I rule! Dave: Amulet pulls out the PPV win, and after some shilling, we’ll hand you back to the RUSH team where the Network Title will be on the line. (NEW to PCWShopzone.com - The Hiro Sautome ‘Legends’ action figure, only $11.99 and a 10% chance it will arrive.) Argyle: Welcome back from the RUSH crew. Raves: HEY! I’m PCW born and bred. Argyle: We have the Network Title match all ready to begin, so we’ll hand you straight to Clay. Gideon Poe vs Matt Hunt (c) Clay: THE FOLLOWING CONTEST IS SCHEDULED FOR ONE FALL AND IS A GERMAN RULES CHRISTMAS LADDER MATCH FOR THE RUSH NETWORK CHAMPIONSHIP! A mixed reaction from the fans, who highly anticipate the match. Argyle: I have a very suspicious feeling about this "German Rules" Kris Raves: Oh calm down, Argie. It's gonna be great! Vanilla Skillz: Hunt's spills that it's "ground breaking" - what a crock, yo. "Wish" by Nine Inch Nails hits the arena to a good ovation from the fans. Clay: INTRODUCING FIRST, THE CHALLENGER: FROM BANGOR, MAINE AND WEIGHING IN AT 235LBS...GIDEON DARAY POE!!! Poe appears at the entrance way and makes his way to the ring. The focused challenger notices the array of horizontal ladders in the aisleway as he approaches the squared circle. Argyle: This has to be Poe's biggest opportunity in RUSH since joining the roster approximately a month ago. Vanilla Skillz: He has to get this match, yo. To show that the resistance ain't gonna drop without a fight! Kris Raves: Pffft, what a load of b.s if I ever heard it. There's an erected ladder a few feet away from the ring, so Poe stops and glances the 20ft object up and down, before swiftly walking past it. Kris Raves: This mooch sure better be aware of what he's getting himself into! Argyle: Well, when the main stipulation of the match hasn't even been announced; you might as well walk into the match blind! Poe slides into the ring to a huge pop from the fans and as "Wish" slowly fades, he limbers up and awaits the champion. Kris Raves: Oh, I can't wait!! What's the stip?!? Vanilla Skillz: I bet Kannon and his group of sell-outs are involved, yo. Argyle: That isn't a bad suggestion, Skillz. Kris Raves: Pffft, as if!! Poe's entrance theme is cut off by complete darkness. Argyle: Come on then, Raves...say it... Kris Raves: Damn right I will...but not just yet! Vanilla Skillz: I got it...get ya'll asses ready for another sellout. The arena is full of booing as the deep German voice comes over the speakers. Zehn...Neun...Acht...Sieben... Argyle: I still can't believe that stunt that Hunt pulled on Fusion. Kris Raves: Hahaha, that was classic. "Merry Christmas from DDT!" Wonderful!! Sechs...Funf...Vier... Vanilla Skillz: It was whack yo, but at least Jenkins ain't no hostage no more. Argyle: My thoughts exactly, although the damage done to the doc's head is too harsh to think of. Zwei...Eins...AUS!! Kris Raves: Here we go, mooches...get ready for das booooooooom! A crescendo of fireworks and pyros explode at the entrance way as Rammestein's "Sonne" bursts out into the arena. Clay: HIS OPPONENT...FROM HAMBURG, GERMANY AND WEIGHING IN AT 290LBS...THE RUSH NETWORK CHAMPION...DAS DANGEROUS TECHNIK, MATT HUNT!!! The jeering and booing is thunderous as Hunt soon appears through all the smoke at the entryway. DDT strolls down the aisleway, with his Network Title firmly resting on his right shoulder and ignores all the heat from the nearby fans on either side. Argyle: I have to admit, Hunt is in great condition. Vanilla Skillz: I wanna know what this stip is, yo. Kris Raves: This is going to be the single most biggest moment in my life...well, before RUSH's courageous owner Mr Kannon hands Duke's ass to him later tonight...and Matthews hands Mullet his ass later tonight...and... Argyle (interutping): Alright, we got it! Vanilla Skillz: "RUSH Owner Xavier Kannon" - that damn near makes me wanna puke, yo. Kris Raves: Well you better hold it up, fool. Hunt also stops when he approaches the upright ladder and he too scans the metal article up and down...but with that smirk on his face. Argyle: Now what the hell is he smirking at? Vanilla Skillz: He's always got that damn grin on his face, yo. Kris Raves: He's probably smiling at the prospect of ramming Poe's face into the ladder again and again and again!!! Argyle: Isn't that surprising? After what seems to be an eternity, Hunt finally moves but instead of walking around the ladder and bends his knees slightly and stupidly jigs his way under the ladder. Argyle: He might as well have smashed a mirror! Vanilla Skillz: What a dumbass, yo. Kris Raves: What? Vanilla Skillz: If you walk under a ladder, you're asking for bad luck, dawg. Kris Raves: Pffft, old wives tale. As a determined Poe stares at Hunt as he enters the ring, a thin round object on the end of a long thick elevator-type cable begins to lower from the rafter. Argyle: Well it seems that this is a traditional Ladder Match. Vanilla Skillz: Nah dawg, I reckon this German Rules is a big deal. Kris Raves: You're damn right it is! Argyle: If that's the case, when the hell are we going to find out that this "German Rules" is? Kris Raves: I...er... Hunt steps into the ring and holds the title aloft with an expression of immense pride. Poe looks on with a disgusted expression as Hunt hands the title to referee Leo Micelli. Argyle: I've noticed that Hunt has been more self conceited since becoming Network Champion. Vanilla Skillz: Nah, it ain't that dawg. It's cos he knows that his job is safe, now that's he's with Kannon. Kris Raves: Well, he isn't exactly "with" Mr Kannon...but oh yeah, his job is safe!! Micelli wraps the title around the circular object before giving the thumbs up. Moments later, the title belt hangs off as it's towed up into the air. Argyle: Well, I'm still waiting for this "German Rules" Kris Raves: All in good time, my dear Argie. Now quit with the whinging. Hunt and Poe watch the title being hoisted away from them. Ref Micelli is then about to signal for the bell when DDT prevents him, before strolling over to the opposite turnbuckle to grab a mic. Kris Raves: This must be it!! Vanilla Skillz: Finally, yo. I think you see where this is going. Crowd: SIX! SEVEN! EIGHT! NINE! TEN! By the time we hit number ten, Stimpo's face under the mask looks like a bruised and bloody mess. He droops to the ground against Harris' entrapment. Tasting the victory, Marvin pops his hips in a little and hurls Stimpo overhead, belly-to-belly style. Argyle: A LIVING DEATH! That's the setup! Stimpo just got hit with A Living Death! As he begins to stand up again, Marvin hears the crowd booing. He's not really sure why, but he checks all his bases to make sure. Ebola III and El Spiffy! are still barely moving amongst the remains of the Spanish announce table. The Spanish announcers are down as well. No one's running through the crowd. And Stimpo lies at a broken pile by his feet. But the crowd is still booing, and he doesn't know why. He turns to demand of them what their problem is…and gets the answer in the form of a steel chair to the face, courtesy one Chris Matthews. Argyle: The hell?!!? Raves: WHOO! It's not just Matthews, mind you. It's also McGlocklan, Mordred and Matt Hunt. The Four M's of RUSH heeldom, if you will. Of course, they're not a sudden alliance or anything. There are other undertones going on. And don't worry, Argyle is just as confused as you are. Argyle: WHAT THE HELL IS GOING ON HERE? WHY ARE THEY ALL OUT HERE WITH CHAIRS? As the four men convene on Marvin Harris, chairs swinging, the PCWTron, and the feed to the crowds at home, switches to a shot backstage. There, Xavier Kannon is watching intently on a monitor, chuckling to himself as he stretches for his own match later on in the show. Raves: Atta boy Xavi! That's my X-man! Argyle: That bastard! He refuses to let the Resistance win, even in matches where his boys aren't even involved! Raves: That's a caring owner right there! Skillz: I ain't nevah seen a bigger hatah in all my life! Well, actually, maybe Quo… Kannon continues to muse at the happenings in the ring as the feed cuts back to the attack. All four men are raining down chairshots on the "Voice of the Resistance," leaving him bloodied and helpless. Matt Hunt raises his chair, preparing for the kill shot…and it's grabbed out of his hands. Raves: Hu? Everyone in the arena suddenly adjusts their vision 4 feet to the right of Hunt, and the man who just saved Harris. Stimpo. Stimpo? Argyle: Stimpo? Raves: STIMPO?!? You heard us right. Stimpo. As the crowd response suddenly switches to cheers for Stimpo's intervention, the luchadore gives Matt Hunt a shove, accosting him for the attack. Hunt says something back to him that can't be picked up by camera mics, then he delivers a low blow that doubles Stimpo over. Boos pick up once again as the other three converge on Stimpo, beating him with the same ruthlessness that they had used on Marvin. Outside the ring, by the commentators table, Bob Charlie doesn't see any of it, as he's checking on the well-being of Ebola III and Spiffy!, who both seem to be mildly concussed at the least. Argyle: This is atrocious! This…this…this mugging is robbing two men of a shot a championship gold, and it's robbing the fans of what was a great match! Raves: Harris had it coming…and from the looks of Stimpo's sackless behavior, he did, too. Skillz: Man, fuck these sellouts, yo. Fuck them, fuck PCW and fuck Xavier Kannon! They'll pay for dis shit. McGlocklan and Matthews pause to hold up Stimpo, blood seeping through his mask. Matt Hunt, retrieving his chair, continues talking to Stimpo, but his words can't be heard over the boos of the crowd. Hunt nods to his partners in crime, who drop Stimpo at the same time. As he falls, Hunt swings the chair, connecting baseball-style with Stimpo's head and leaving him out cold in a pile on the mat. Satisfied with their work, the sellouts take their chairs and retreat through the crowd before any Resistance members could get to them. Outside the ring, Bob Charlie is finally content with the status of the other two men. He rolls in just in time to see Marvin and Stimpo, both bloody and unconscious, lying next to each other. Argyle: This is a crime. This is unjust. Raves: This is Sports Entertainment, baby! Better deal with it! Not having a damn clue why either man is in the state they are in, Charlie has only one option, and he exercises it. He counts. 1… 2… Argyle: No. Not like this! 3… Raves: Rules are rules, Argie… 4… 5… Skillz: Man, dat's bullshit, yo. 6… 7… 8… Neither man moves. EMTs are already sprinting down the aisle towards the ring. 9… Argyle: Goddamn them. Goddamn every one of them. 10. Bob Charlie calls for the bell. The ringside crew is unsure of what to do in the situation, so not much happens after that bell. Raves: What's the decision? Argyle: I don't think they know. Hell, Double Jeopardy matches are a rarity as is, there's not too much precedent for variations. Skillz: Well, except for that PCW match next, yo. Argyle: Well, yeah. If the Treble Jeopardy match that was booked long after Matt had this DJ idea cooked up suffers a similar problem, then they can thank Matt for setting the precedent. [/making his point.] Raves: Size of Melons because he can't get them pumped by any chicks! [/retort =P] Ebola III is first to his feet outside, not surprisingly. Grabbing El Spiffy! by his mask, he drags the so-called 'disgrace' back to the ring and throws him in. Argyle: I'm amazed El Spiffy! is even moving by now. Raves: He's only moving because Ebola III wants him to do it. He'll stop when the champ says so. Ebola whips El Spiffy! into the ropes and takes off to the adjacent side. He meets him in the middle with a jumping calf kick that floors the Spiffster. Capitalizing, he pulls thel uchadore right back up into another Irish whip. This time he catches El Spiffy! off the ropes, spins, and drives him down with a wicked spinebuster. Upon impact, he rolls through and back to his feet. Raves: Quarantine! Argyle: The night could be shorter than expected for El Spiffy!... El Spiffy! writhes in pain on the mat, but he doesn't stay still. Eager to put the match to bed, Ebola pulls him back up and goes for another Irish whip. This time around, however, El Spiffy! reverses it. Ebola III is tossed into the ropes, and El Spiffy! follows him, but he jumps onto the nearby turnbuckle. Grabbing the back of Ebola's mask, he springs backwards, driving the champion face-first into the mat. Argyle: Chihuahua headlock! These two are trading their signatures! Ebola is stunned, enough for El Spiffy! to mount the ropes. He looks down onto Ebola, and swallows hard. Argyle: Uh-oh… Raves: Goddammit… Skillz: Hellz yeah! The murmur in the crowd acknowledges what's coming as well. In desperation, El Spiffy! jumps off the top rope, somersaulting in midair as he flails his arms and legs wildly. Argyle: BORDER JUMP! Well, not exactly. See, that WAS the Border Jump setup, the reckless flipping senton. But to call it the Border Jump would imply that he, well…hit the move. He didn't. Ebola rolls out of the way in the nick of time as El Spiffy! slams into the mat flat on his back. He arcs a little, in visible pain from the move, then sags back. Ebola III is quick to cover. 1… . . . 2… . . . 3NO!!!! One shoulder separates them from the end of the match. One shoulder shooting off the mat. Argyle: El Spiffy!'s hanging on for dear life! Raves: Can we just end this? Doesn't Ebola have some more mist, or maybe a fireball or something? Ebola III pulls the Spiffster up and locks in a front facelock. He hoists El Spiffy! into the air for a vertical suplex. He walks to the nearby turnbuckle, setting up perpendicular and preparing to drop for a falcon arrow. But before he can, El Spiffy! wriggles free, falling out of the hold and to his feet, but keeping the ¾-turn facelock! Skillz: Aw naw, yo! As Ebola is still caught by the reversal, El Spiffy! keeps hold of his head and runs, scampering up the turnbuckle and pushing off into the air! Argyle: Here it comes! Raves: No, dammit, no! Cameras flash across the arena once again, everyone fighting to catch a still photo of the move. El Spiffy! kicks his legs into the open air, keeping hold of Ebola III as the champion spins, helpless. Then, El Spiffy! scissors his legs and rotates, turning his ¾-turn facelock into a front facelock, and effectively turning the Acid Drop into a tornado DDT. Argyle: Spiffy! DDT!!! Raves: NO! He spins down to execute the move. The crowd braces for the impact…but it never comes. Because El Spiffy! never got to the DDT. He was caught right as he tried to get to the tornado part. As he spun down, Ebola's free arms shot up, one to tuck El Spiffy!'s head, and the other to block his legs before he could swing them down. A mass of limbs, the two men spin in the middle of the ring, El Spiffy! perched on Ebola III's shoulders. Flashbulbs continue to pop, the crowd hanging on every second. As they complete one 360-degree spin, Ebola starts another, then drops, pitching towards the mat and taking El Spiffy! down with him in a Death Valley Driver. They hit together, Ebola III with the clean-cut DVD, and El Spiffy! planted in the center of the ring. There are no wrestler-specific crowd reactions anymore. It's just one solid cheer for everything the crowd sees. And the reversal to end all reversals is yet another reason for them to bellow in delight at the match in front of them. Ebola III rolls up for a second shaking off the dizziness from the spin. Then, he flops onto a lateral press, hooking a leg and pulling it all the way up as Bob Charlie counts. 1… . . 2… . . And we find one of those moments where a second lasts for an eternity. Fortunately for Ebola, he was willing to wait for an eternity. Even more fortunately, El Spiffy! wouldn't be moving for one. 3!!!! Raves: YES! Argyle: Ebola III was defeated El Spiffy!! His first fall has ended! Skillz: But what about the second fall, yo? The deafening crowd roar for the culmination of the match isn't enough to dissuade Bob Charlie from doing his job. Remembering that in the heat of the moment, he had never cleared up the issue with Stimpo and Mavin Harris, slides out of the ring. Skipping over the mess left by the Spanish announce table, Charlie makes his way over to the timekeeper and Clay Clayborne. Argyle: It looks like Bob is going to clear this all up for us. Skillz: It doesn't look like Ebola III could stand another match, yo. The crowd doesn't seem to be letting up on that pop any time soon. El Spiffy!, spread eagle in the middle of the mat, has his eyes opened, but isn't moving an inch. Ebola III is sprawled out right next to him, hands over his face, breathing heavily as he soaks in the response. Raves: What the hell's the decision, huh? Let's get on with it! Charlie, finally done talking with Clay, grabs the Syndicate title and heads for the ring. He slips in under the ropes as Ebola III manages to climb, slowly, to his feet. Angered more than usual (well, actually, that's just casual Satanic violence; go figure) Clyde mounts El Diablo's chest and rapidly punches Diablo in the face with straight fists to the eyes, mouth, and nose. Liebig threatens to disqualify him for use of the clenched fists. Clyde: What the hell? Liebig: *Shrugs* Clyde stands up and scoops up Liebig, throwing him out of the ring. Clyde: Fuck you, enforcing the rules! Ellie: Here comes the splat! Clyde turns around. *CRIK!* Crowd: DAMN! Leveled with the sledgehammer shot to the head, Clyde is out on the mat. His head is bleeding from the compound fracture of the skull that pierced the skin. El Diablo holds the red sledgehammer in his hands, staring at the weighted hammer. His eyes are wide-open, evil in his eyes. And he takes the hammer and beats Clyde's chest in with wide swings of it. Jeff: El Diablo could kill Clyde! Ellie: It wouldn't be much of a loss. A headshot. Pounding into the ribs. El Diablo swings as fast and hard as he can to drive Clyde's inner organs out of his body. Jeff: Florien Liebig is still out on the floor, he can't call this DQ! Ellie: Good, I was tired of Clyde as champ. Dragging Clyde back over to the corner by choking him with the handle, Diablo sits Clyde on the top rope facing forward. He places the weight of the hammer on Clyde's nose and yanks him down off of the top rope. Jeff: Super Facebuster using the sledgehammer! Clyde's face is pouring red from his nose, mouth, and the forehead. El Diablo tosses the sledgehammer aside and rubs his hands in Clyde's blood. Ellie: Even I'll agree, that's just sick. Diablo takes the blood and licks it off his hands. Dave: Ewwwwzzies.... Kicking the sledgehammer out of the ring, El Diablo just stands over Clyde, wiping Clyde's blood all over his body while the pool forms underneath the Burned Man. Jeff: This is the worst thing since the New Breed title-swapping! El Diablo decides on more punishment and goes outside to get a chair. Sliding it into the ring, he sets it up unfolded beside Clyde's broken body. BRAIN DISORDER! Dave: THE PHARAOH! Jeff: Didn't we fire him? Ellie: Nah, much as I'd have liked that the Golden Hummer didn't get to him. Jeff: THE PHARAOH RETURNS AND SPIKES EL DIABLO ONTO THE OPEN CHAIR WITH THE BRAIN DISORDER! A standing ovation, a loud, deafening cheer for Pharaoh Titu Xansiv as he shouts at El Diablo, who lay fallen with his head draped on the heavily dented chair. Jeff: It was El Diablo that injured Pharaoh shortly after Red October, and he is back and BOY IS HE ANGRY! Ellie: This sand-sucking pansy better not stay for long... Or he'll have the wrath of UPPER-LEVEL HEEL to deal with! Pharaoh pulls Diablo to his feet again and brings him up over the chair again. He stalls in the belly-to-back cradle tombstone position before DRILLING EL DIABLO INTO THE CHAIR SO HARD THAT IT SNAPS IN TWO! Jeff: JEWEL OF THE NILE! JEWEL OF THE NILE! Pharaoh drags Clyde's lifeless body onto El Diablo and rolls out of the ring, reviving Liebig with a kick to the ribs and tossing him into the ring to make the count. Ellie: NOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!! Jeff: ONE!!!! Liebig: TWO!!!! Crowd: THREE!!!!!!! Dave: This isn't, like, THAT exciting. The bell rings and Florien Liebig goes out to retrieve the belt. The ring commentators revert to their original personalities. Dave: Clyde retains! Clyde retains! Jeff: BAH~! Ellie: Like, disqualification! Stuff! STUFF! Clay: THE WINER OF THIS MATCH.... AND STILL PCW WORLD HEAVYWEIGHT CHAMPION.... CLYDE!!! Dave: The match is over, there is no disqualification! Ellie: Urgh! Clyde shakily gets to his feet, his entire body covered in his own blood, and is handed his title. Dave: And he's regained his belt as well! It's about time. Jeff: Oh, burn in Hell already. As the burned man raises the belt overhead to an overwhelming pop from the crowd, Pharaoh rolls into the ring again. Jeff: You already screwed El Diablo out of the World Title, haven't you done enough? Pharaoh stands over the fallen Diablo with his hands on his hips. He talks down to him, as Clyde walks over to his side and joins in. Ellie: This is like, so not fair! Dave: The hell it isn't, he had it coming! Clyde and Pharaoh talk trash to El D....the champ then looks up. He turns his glance to Pharaoh, smiles broadly....then BASHES HIM IN THE FOREHEAD WITH THE WORLD TITLE BELT!!!!! Dave: WHAT THE HELL!?!? Jeff: SWEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEERVE! Pharaoh drops like a ton of bricks, clutching his head! Clyde raises his belt again, using the tape he had on his wrists to tie it like a glove to his hand, then CRASHES IT DOWN ON HIM AGAIN AND AGAIN AND AGAIN!!!!! Dave: Clyde is beating the hell out of a defenseless Pharaoh!!!!! WHY!?!? Pharaoh tries to roll away, but the champ follows and continues smashing the solid gold title over and over and over into his head!!!!! He then rolls out of the ring, pulling up the tarp and going underneath. Ellie: This is like, so weirdly cool! Dave: What now!? Clyde produces a steel chair, which he throws inside the ring. Then a second....then, he drags out what appears to be a large board with several nasty looking nails sticking out of it. Jeff: THE SCOURGE KILLER!!!!! Dave: You hated Clyde's guts all of five minutes ago, now you're rooting for him!? Jeff: I knew about this all along! Dave: (Sighs) The burned man heads back into the ring, just as Pharaoh stumbles to his feet. Clyde grabs a chair, sticks the edge in Pharaoh's windpipe, then SHOVES HIM STRAIGHT DOWN INTO IT THROAT FIRST!!!!!! Jeff: ASPYXIATOR!!!!!! Ellie: Ew, this is like SO gonna be messy! Clyde lays the nail studded board down, then drags Pharaoh up by the hair. He holds him close, leering, then yanks him into a powerbomb position and moves him over to the board!!!!! Dave: HE'LL KILL HIM!!!!! NO!!!!!! The lights suddenly go out in the arena. Jeff: Huh? At the top of the entryway a burst of flame shoots up. It lights things up slightly, just enough to reveal a leather clad biker wearing a black helmet. Ellie: It's....it's.... Jeff: IT'S GHOST RIDER COME BACK TO KILL US ALL!!!!! Dave: (Sighs) In the ring, Clyde lets Pharaoh fall limply and stares wide eyed at the interloper. The mystery rider revves his engine, then roars towards the ring!!!!!! Dave: We've seen this once before!!!! Jeff: And it ended up in Clyde being killed!!!! RUN!!!!! The biker reaches the ring, circling around it. Carefully the demon watches, as finally the man stops. Slowly he climbs off, rolling into the ring and marching straight up to Clyde. Dave: Oh my.... Clyde stares into the motorcyclist's visor....who reaches up and throws his helmet off to reveal none other than Darkstar as the crowd explodes!!!!!! Dave: DARKSTAR!!!!! DARKSTAR HAS COME TO FINISH THAT BACKSTABBING DEMON ONCE AND FOR ALL!!!!!! Ellie: Like, YOU URINATING ON ME FREAK!!!!!! Darkstar locks eyes with Clyde, kicking the bed of nails out of the ring with one foot. The demon returns his gaze with an expression of shocked amazement....the Dream King then reaches into his pocket, pulling out a massive chain. Clyde stares at it as DS snaps the object off the ground, cracking it like a bullwhip!!!!! Dave: That very chain dragged Clyde to his screaming death once!!!!!! Jeff: He didn't scream! And it'll never happen again! KILL HIM CLYDE!!!!!! KILL HIM DEAD!!!!!! Still not taking his eyes off of Clyde, Darkstar pulls his arm back, readying the chain!!!!! The champ stands transfixed.....then DS turns, WHIPPING A STANDING PHARAOH RIGHT IN THE FACE!!!!!!! Jeff: SWEEEEEEEEEEEEEEERVE NUMBER TWO!!!!!! Dave: NO!!!!! NO!!!!! NO!!!!! NO!!!!! Pharaoh clutches his forehead which splits in a shower of crimson!!!!! Clyde and Darkstar follow him, beating him into a bloody pulp!!!!!! Ellie: Like, WOW!!!!! Clyde grabs Pharaoh by the hands, holding him down while Darkstar whips him over and over with the chain!!!!! Meanwhile El Diablo very groggily stands.....takes a look over at the carnage, then shakes his head and rolls out of the ring. Dave: What a coward!!!!! Jeff: He's a smart man!!!!! Frustrated, El D slaps the mat and backs up the ramp. In the ring Darkstar continues to whip the hell out of Pharaoh, while Clyde hunches over him and grinds the rough edge of his title belt into the gaping wound on his forehead!!!!! Dave: Sick!!!! SICK!!!!!! Pharaoh tries to get away on his knees, but Clyde bashes him in the back of the head with the title once again!!!!! He then looks up, smiling. Jeff: Mwahaha! Ellie: I can't see, what the hell is that? High up above, a form moves in the rafters. It seemingly fiddles with something.....then a second massive chain begins to descend. Dave: Oh my God. Down below Pharaoh again tries to stand, but Darkstar hooks him in a full nelson, then splatters him with the PRESSURE DROP!!!!!! Jeff: Mwahahahahahaha! Pharaoh twitches spastically.....Clyde flips him over, then kneels while Darkstar hands him a crowbar from his jacket, and digs the edge of the crowbar into the cut on his forehead, ripping it open even further!!!!! Ellie: Nasty! Hehe! Pharaoh begins gushing blood, as the chain finally reaches terra firma and we see it is in fact a noose. Both DS and Clyde drag their victim up, walking him over to the chain and shoving his head into the loop of it. Jeff: About to get nastier! Above, the maniacal and shrill laughter of Bethany rips through the blackness. Slowly the horrible metal noose begins to rise, closing around Pharaoh's throat and dragging him off his feet!!!!!! Dave: SOMEONE STOP THIS!!!!!! As Pharaoh rises, Darkstar and Clyde take the pair of chairs and bash him right in the face with it, reducing his visage into a bloody pulp!!!!! Up, up he goes, kicking his feet.....then this ceases as well, he hangs limply. Dave: Oh my God. Jeff: Shut it Kern! Titu's blood literally drips down to the canvas. Clyde takes his newly reclaimed title belt, walks beneath Pharaoh and holds it overhead, allowing the crimson to fall onto it. Dave: In all my years, I cannot believe what I am seeing here. Jeff: Hehehe! Dave: Darkstar and Clyde formed the original Dark Age just over a year and a half ago. Through that time they've been responsible for some of the most gruesome things I personally have ever witnessed.... Ellie: Like Stryfe as world champion! Dave: .....yes, like Stryfe as world champion. But this time they've gone too far.....this is a reign of blood! A godforsaken reign of blood!!!!! Jeff: MWAHAHAHAHA!!!!!! Clyde's gold title belt now stands completely covered in red. The blood of the Pharaoh runs off it, as he smiles sickly. Dave: We're out of time here! Jeff: You're all out of time here! MWAHAHA!!!!! Dave: (Staring) For myself, Ellie and this PSYCHO next to me..... Jeff: DIEDIEDIEDIEDIEDIEDIEDIE!!!!!! Dave: Ugh! We'll see you on Tuesday!!!!! Darkstar smirks up at the bleeding Pharaoh, Bethany's hysterical laughter rings out, and Clyde continues to let the blood fall onto both him, his belt and the ring as the PCW logo and copyright appear, and we fade to black.
